## Explicitly untested dimensions

These are registered gaps, not negative findings:

- **listing-age-v1** — newly listed markets may anchor inefficiently before information and liquidity accumulate *Status: not run; folds and FDR required.*
- **recurring-series-v1** — persistent series-specific participant composition may create repeatable residual pricing *Status: not run; folds and hierarchical search correction required.*
- **ladder-monotonicity-v1** — ordered strikes may violate probability monotonicity *Status: blocked by missing historical strike metadata.*
- **linked-contract-lead-lag-v1** — information may propagate slowly between related outcomes in the same event *Status: blocked by incomplete historical relationship semantics.*
- **activity-staleness-flow-v1** — activity, stale prices, and recent aggressor imbalance may condition structural mispricing *Status: not run; folds and expanded-search FDR required.*
- **resolution-objectivity-v1** — judgment-heavy settlement language may command a risk premium or create avoidable losses *Status: blocked by missing historical rule metadata.*
